Gross pay minus base minus overtime captures longevity, stipends, retroactive contract settlements and leave buyouts together — the Gross Earnings report does not separate them. A retro settlement landing in someone’s final year looks identical here to a leave payout.","Most separations are unremarkable. The median separation year’s residual is small; this is a tail, and the tail is what the totals are made of.","A separation year can be a partial year. 2d:["$","ul",null,{"style":{"margin":"0 0 10px","paddingLeft":20,"display":"grid","gap":6},"children":[["$","li",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Names, titles and pay are public."}]," ",["$","a",null,{"href":"https://www.nysenate.gov/legislation/laws/PBO/87","style":{"color":"var(--rbl-link)"},"children":"Public Officers Law § 87(3)(b)"}]," requires every agency to keep “","a record setting forth the name, public office address, title and salary of every officer or employee of the agency",".” That is all the table shows about a person."]}],["$","li",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"What police records must leave out, this table never carries."}]," ",["$","a",null,{"href":"https://www.nysenate.gov/legislation/laws/PBO/89","style":{"color":"var(--rbl-link)"},"children":"Public Officers Law § 89(2-b)"}],": “","a law enforcement agency shall redact the following information",".” ","Before releasing police disciplinary records, an agency must remove medical history, home addresses, personal phone numbers and e-mail addresses, Social Security numbers, and use of employee assistance or counseling services. The same subdivision says it does not override the other laws that make “title, salary, and dates of employment” public. This table shows none of the redacted kinds of information."," Each quotation is also checked for Social Security numbers, phone numbers and e-mail addresses, and any found are removed."]}],["$","li",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Charges are not findings."}]," ",["$","a",null,{"href":"https://www.nysenate.gov/legislation/laws/CVS/75","style":{"color":"var(--rbl-link)"},"children":"Civil Service Law § 75(2)"}],": “","The burden of proving incompetency or misconduct shall be upon the person alleging the same.","” ",["$","a",null,{"href":"https://www.nysenate.gov/legislation/laws/CVS/75","style":{"color":"var(--rbl-link)"},"children":"Civil Service Law § 75(3)"}]," allows a suspension without pay pending a hearing “","may be suspended without pay for a period not exceeding thirty days",",” and under ",["$","a",null,{"href":"https://www.nysenate.gov/legislation/laws/TWN/155","style":{"color":"var(--rbl-link)"},"children":"Town Law § 155"}]," a town police officer who is not convicted “","he shall be entitled to full pay from the date of suspension",".”"]}],["$","li",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Only the Town’s words are quoted; the name is reviewed."}]," ",["$","a",null,{"href":"https://www.nysenate.gov/legislation/laws/CVR/74","style":{"color":"var(--rbl-link)"},"children":"Civil Rights Law § 74"}]," protects a fair and true report of an official proceeding, but “","This section does not apply to a libel contained in any other matter added by any person concerned in the publication",".” The resolution text here is the Town’s own; the name is this site’s match. So no name is shown until a person has checked it, and each check lapses after three to six months."]}]]}]
2e:["$","p",null,{"style":{"margin":"0 0 8px"},"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"How each match is reviewed."}]," Before a name is shown, a reviewer confirms that the file number is on the roster once; that the person’s payroll title and department fit the resolution; that the quotation is the resolution’s own words with nothing the law requires removed; that any later resolution about the same number has been read; and that the note on what the action means is right. A new match from the meeting records shows no name until then. When a later resolution, such as a determination of charges, cites the same number, it appears in that row."]}]
